Let's derive fast exact-ish score:
```
def convolve(a): return np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
def score_fast(a): n=a.size; c=np.convolve(a,a); S=a.sum(); if S<0.01: inf; return 2*n*c.max()/S*S
```
For many iterations, use FFT:
```
def conv_fft(a):
 n=a.size; L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
 fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
```
Numerical may have small negatives/roundoff; use for approximate. For gradient, need approx active weights from Fast conv. We can threshold.

Algorithm pseudo:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
  # use np.convolve for n<=300? else FFT
  L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
  c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
  return c

def fast_score(c):
  n=c.size? nseq; maxc=c.max(); S=1 if normalized return 2*n*maxc
```
Normalize a after each step. Compute p=conv/a.sum^2? If normalized sum1, p=conv.

def active_gradient(a,w,method='fft'):
  n=len(a)
  if n small: use loops? O(n*active) maybe
  # w length 2n-1
  # g_i = sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}
  # using convolution:
  w_rev = w[::-1]
  conv = np.convolve(a, w_rev, mode='full') # length 3n-2
  g = conv[2*n-2 : 3*n-2] ??? Need verify.
```

Let's design final code with a fairly aggressive local search and multi-start. Need ensure time. Let's outline code functions:

```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
except: ...

def safe_parent():
  h = height_sequence_1 if in globals() else None
  validate; clip [0,1000]; if sum>0 return normalized maybe; else None

def fast_convolve(a):
  n=a.size
  if n <= 300: return np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
  L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
  c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
  return c
```
Need for a normalized sum1; p=conv (since S=1). For unnormalized candidate use score_fast.
```
def normalize(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float)
  a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
  s=a.sum()
  if s <=1e-12: return None
  return a/s
```

`active_weights(p, n, mode)`:
- pmax = p.max()
- if not finite: None
- weights = np.zeros(2n-1)
- Option 'qp': threshold = pmax*(1 - frac). frac list [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]. active = p >= threshold. w=active * p? Or active_p = np.maximum(p - (pmax - frac*pmax),0). If sum zero, w=[0..,1,top].
- Also 'softmax': beta = B/pmax; shift=p-pmax; w=exp(min(beta*shift, 0)); normalize.
Because p values ~1/n, beta maybe 50/pmax? Let's set B options [5,20,100]. `w = exp(np.clip(beta*(p-pmax), -50, 0)); w/=w.sum()`. If B=5, broad; 100 narrow.

`gradient_from_w(a,w)`:
- Use FFT if n>200 else direct. Direct: g_i = ∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. For n small, can vectorize: build indices? For n<=200 O(n^2)=40k okay.
```
def active_grad_direct(a,w):
  n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
  # for j, add a_j * w[j:j+n] to g? g_i += a_j w_{i+j}. For fixed j, i from0..n-1, w index i+j from j..j+n-1.
  for j in range(n): g += a[j]*w[j:j+n]
  return g
```
This O(n^2) python loop; for n=200 fine. For n=1000 loop 1000*1000 addition in C? Each g += slice in Python n times, maybe okay (1e6 per iter) but 1000 iterations 1e9. Use FFT for n>100.

FFT gradient:
```
def active_grad_fft(a,w):
  n=len(a)
  L=1 << (3*n-1).bit_length() # full length 3n-2
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
  fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L)
  c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,L)
  g=c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
  g[np.abs(g)<1e-15]=0
  return g
```
Need ensure w length 2n-1. Good. We can precompute reversed w each iteration. FFT length power of two. For n=4096 L next pow2 12288? 3n-2=12286, L=16384. Good.

`score_fast(a, conv=None)`:
- if conv is None conv=fast_convolve(a)
- S=a.sum(); if S<eps inf
- maxc=conv.max()
- return 2*a.size*maxc/(S*S)
But if a normalized, S=1.

`project_simplex(a)`:
Standard:
```
def project(v):
  v=np.asarray(v,float)
  u=np.sort(v[::-1])
  cssm=np.cumsum(u)
  rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,v.size+1)) > (cssm-1))[0][-1]
  theta=(cssm[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
  return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Then normalize. Only used for linear step.

`mirror_step(a, g, scale)`:
```
def single_mirr(a,g,scale,factor):
  a_new = a * np.exp(-factor * g/scale)
  s=a_new.sum(); if s<=0: a_new = a.copy()
  return a_new/s
```
Maybe allow a minimum floor? To avoid underflow, `np.clip(-factor*g/scale, -30, 30)`. Also if any a becomes extremely small, okay.

Optimizer `run_optimize(a0, seconds, rng, final_n_fixed=False)`:
- a = normalize(a0); best=a; best_score=score_fast(a)
- Choose beta_B or frac sequence dynamically.
- Iters limit based on n: `max_iters = int(seconds*1000/(estimate_per_iter))`. We can time few iterations to estimate. Simpler: loop while time < stop, with max maybe 5000.

def opt_mirror(a, seconds, rng):
  a=normalize(a); best_score=score_fast(a); best=a.copy()
  p=fast_convolve(a); pmax=p.max()
  no_imp=0
  it=0
  t0=time.monotonic()
  # adaptive beta_factor
  beta_factors=[10,30,100]
  fracs=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]
  directions = list of factors
  while time < t0+seconds:
    p=fast_convolve(a)
    pmax=p.max()
    if not finite: break
    # choose mode every some it
    mode = beta_factors[it % len]
    if mode < 10? use active frac? Let's use parameter `mode = it % 3`:
      mode0: softmax broad B=10
      mode1: B=50
      mode2: top frac 0.1
    w=...
    g=active_grad(a,w)
    gscale = max(g.max(), 1e-15)
    improved=False
    # Try factors descending, also maybe negative? no
    for fac in [0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       a2 = a * np.exp(-fac*g/gscale)
       a2=normalize(a2)
       sc=score_fast(a2,p? no conv needed compute)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           a=a2; best=a2.copy; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
    if not improved:
       # try linear projection with small lr
       for lr in [0.1,0.5,1]:
          v = a - lr * g/gscale
          v = project_simplex(v); v=normalize(v)
          sc=score_fast(v)
          if sc<best_score -1e-12: accept
       if not improved:
          no_imp+=1
          if no_imp>3:
             # perturb
             sigma=0.1+0.1*np.minimum(no_imp//3,3)
             a = perturb(a,sigma,rng)
             sc=score_fast(a)
             # accept perturbation even if worse if far from best? It must have a to continue. We can keep a as perturbed but best remains.
             # Also maybe if perturbed better update.
             no_imp=0
    it+=1
  return best,best_score
```

LP function:
```
def lp_direction(a):
  n=len(a); c=a; M=max(convolve(a))
  A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
  for k in range(2*n-1):
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    # A[k, lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi : k-lo+1]? Need coefficient for g_j: f*g coefficient k = ∑_{j=lo}^{hi} f_{k-j} g_j.
    # If j from lo..hi, idx_f=k-j. So row = a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]? Let's check length hi-lo+1, j=lo -> f k-lo; j=hi -> f k-hi. Row order j increasing: f[k-lo], f[k-lo-1],...,f[k-hi] = reversed slice a[k-hi+1:k-lo+1]?
    row = a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]? For k-lo=hi? Let's do small n=3,k=2: lo=0,hi=2. Need coefficients for g0,g1,g2: a2,a1,a0. a[k-hi=0 : k-lo+1=3] = a[0:3]=[a0,a1,a2], reversed ->[a2,a1,a0] correct. For k=1: lo0,hi1: a[0:2] [a0,a1] reversed [a1,a0] correct. Good.
    A[k,lo:hi+1]=a[lo? Actually k-hi : k-lo+1]
  c_obj = -np.ones(n) - 1e-8 * rng.normal(size=n) maybe ensure sum maximize.
  res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=(0,10.0), method='highs')
  if not success or res.x is None: return None
  g=res.x; G=sum(g); if G <=1+1e-9: return None
  g = g/G # sum1
  return g
```

```

Let's implement perturbation:
```
def perturb(a, sigma, rng):
  b=a.copy()
  # log-perturb block
  l = size maybe
  width=max(1,int(len(b)*rng.uniform(0.01,0.25)))
  s=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
  b[s:s+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma, size=width))
  # occasionally global trend
  if rng.random()<0.3:
     t=np.linspace(0,1,n); drift=np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma/2)*t) or sign
     b*=drift
  return normalize(b)
```
Also reverse `b=a[::-1]`.

Let's tune active weights:
```
def make_weights(p, mode):
  m=p.max(); m=max(m,1e-300)
  if mode ==0: # softmax B=20
    B=20
  elif mode ==1: B=80
  else: B=200
  w=np.exp(np.clip(B*(p-m), -50,0))
  s=w.sum()
  if s<=0: top=argmax; w[top]=1; return w
  return w/s
```

```
def normalize(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
  if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.ones(max(2,a.size))/...
  a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
  return a/s
```

Let's design `optimize_vector` more concretely:
```
def optimize_vector(a0, time_limit, rng, use_lp=False):
    a=normalize(a0)
    if a is None or a.size==0: return None, inf
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_s=score_normalized(a)
    current=a.copy(); cur_s=best_s
    it=0; noimp=0
    t_start=time.monotonic(); stop=t_start+max(0.1,time_limit)
    # estimate score exact?
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        p=fast_convolve(current)
        pmax=p.max()
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax<=0:
            # reset
            current=best.copy(); break
        # choose mode
        mode = it % 3
        w=make_weights(p, [5,20,80][mode])
        g=active_gradient(current, w)
        gm=g.max()
        if gm<=0 or not finite:
            g=np.zeros_like(current)
        improved=False
        # factor choices vary with noimp
        factors = [0.1, 0.3, 1.0]
        if noimp>10: factors=[0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0,2.0]
        gn = g/gm if gm>0 else g
        for fac in factors:
            # exponential step
            z = -fac * gn
            z = np.clip(z, -30, 30)
            cand = current * np.exp(z)
            cand=normalize(cand)
            if cand is None: continue
            sc=score_normalized(cand, fast_convolve(cand))
            if sc < cur_s - 1e-12 or sc < best_s - 1e-12: # update current? If sc < cur_s
                current=cand; cur_s=sc; improved=True
                if sc < best_s:
                    best=cand.copy(); best_s=sc; noimp=0
                break
        if not improved:
            # try linear projection
            for lr in [0.05,0.2,0.5]:
                v = current - lr*gn
                v = project_simplex(v)
                v=normalize(v)
                sc=score_normalized(v, fast_convolve(v))
                if sc < cur_s - 1e-12:
                   current=v; cur_s=sc; improved=True
                   if sc<best_s: best=v.copy(); best_s=sc; noimp=0
                   break
            if not improved:
                noimp += 1
                if noimp % 5 == 0:
                    # mix current with best
                    if noimp > 10:
                        current = normalize(0.4*current + 0.6*best)
                        cur_s = score_normalized(current, fast_convolve(current))
                    current = perturb(current, sigma=min(0.6, 0.05*noimp), rng)
                    cur_s=score_normalized(current, fast_convolve(current))
                    if cur_s < best_s:
                        best=current.copy(); best_s=cur_s
                    # if launch, maybe reverse orientation
                    if rng.random()<0.1:
                        rev=best[::-1]; rs=score_normalized(rev, fast_convolve(rev))
                        if rs<best_s: best=rev; best_s=rs
                if use_lp and n<=300 and (it % 40 ==0) and noimp>10:
                    gdir=lp_direction(current, rng)
                    if gdir is not None:
                       for mix in [0.05,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0]:
                           cand=normalize((1-mix)*current + mix*gdir)
                           sc=score_normalized(cand, fast_convolve(cand))
                           if sc<cur_s -1e-12:
                               current=cand; cur_s=sc; improved=True
                               if sc<best_s: best=cand.copy(); best_s=sc
                               break
        it +=1
    return best, best_s
```

Let's write `make_weight` code:
```
def make_weights(p, mode):
    m = float(np.max(p))
    if m<=0 or not finite: w=zeros; w[0]=1; return
    B = [1.0,5.0,25.0][mode%3]
    e = -B*(m-p)/m
    e = np.clip(e,-50,0)
    w=np.exp(e)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: top=argmax; w[top]=1
    else: w/=s
    return w
```

```
def score_fast_array(a, conv=None):
  S=a.sum(); if S<... inf
  if conv is None: conv=fast_convolve(a)
  m=conv.max()
  return 2*a.size*float(m)/(S*S)
```
This is exact formula (if conv is exact or approximate). Good.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```

`parent_sequence`:
```
def parent_sequence():
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s>1e-12 and arr.size>0:
       return arr/s
    return None
```

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=int(n)
    m=a.size
    if m==n: return normalize_sequence(a)
    if m==0 or n<=0: return None
    if m>n:
       # average blocks
       usable = (m//n)*n
       if usable <=0:
          a2=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,m), a)
       else:
          tail=a[usable:]
          b=np.zeros(n)
          base=a[:usable].reshape(n, -1)  # Wait reshape(n, -1) if order? contiguous n blocks? Need chunks of length m//n along axis: shape (-1, block).mean(axis=0)
          b=base.mean(axis=0)
          if tail.size:
             # distribute tail by blocks? simple append to first len?
             extra=tail.sum()/n
             b+=extra
       return normalize_sequence(b)
    else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m,endpoint=True)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=True)
       b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
       return normalize_sequence(b)
```
Check reshape: If m=10,n=4, usable=8, a[:8].reshape(-1,2) -> 4x2, mean. Good.

`fast_convolve`:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    if n <= 800:
       return np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
    L=1
    need=2*n-1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    c=c[:need]
    # remove tiny negative
    c[np.minimum(c,0.0)?? Use np.where(c<0,0,c)
    return c
```
Using np.clip may allocate. `c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)`. For exact n<=800 no negative.

For candidate score using FFT, negative small removed; okay.

`score_fast_array`:
```
def score_fast_array(a, conv=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
   S=float(a.sum())
   if n==0 or S<=1e-300: return inf
   if conv is None: conv=fast_convolve(a)
   if conv.size==0: return inf
   m=float(np.max(conv))
   if not np.isfinite(m) or m<=0: return inf
   return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S))
```

`project_simplex`: Need robust.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssm=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u*rho > (cssm-1.0)
    if not np.any(cond): return np.zeros(n)
    ind=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssm[ind]-1.0)/(ind+1.0)
    proj=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    return proj
```
Then normalize.

`active_gradient`:
```
def active_gradient(a,w):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if n <= 200:
       g=np.zeros(n)
       # loop j
       for j in range(n):
          coeff=a[j]
          if coeff !=0:
             g += coeff * w[j:j+n]
       return g
    L=1
    need=3*n-2 # conv length n+(2n-1)-1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,L)
    g=c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    g=np.where(g>0.0,g,0.0)
    return g
```
Check slice endpoint: For n=2, full conv length4, slice 1:3 -> ok. For n=1, need=1? 3*-? If n=1, fast? handle n=1. score for n=1 return 2? But we can avoid n=1. In code, if n==1: g=[w0?]. active_g = a0*w0? Since p0=a0^2. For n=1, w length1. return w[0]*a. But not needed.

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a,sigma,rng):
   n=a.size
   b=a.copy()
   if n==1: return b
   # block log-normal
   width=int(max(1, min(n, rng.integers(1, max(2, n//20)))))? Need integers size.
   width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n//50), max(2, n//5)))
   width=min(width,n)
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
   b[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width))
   if rng.random()<0.4:
      t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      drift=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.7)*t)  # linear exponent
      b *= drift
   if rng.random()<0.15:
      # random oscillatory modest
      b *= 1.0 + 0.25*sigma*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(1,5)*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   b=np.maximum(b,0)
   return normalize_sequence(b)
```

`make_weights` as above.

`lp_direction`:
Need `linprog` not None. Use dense. Limit n<=350 to keep matrix small. Also manage time; if binprog too slow, may block. Set n<=256? We can allow 300. Use `method='highs'` if available. `linprog` signature.
```
def lp_direction(a, rng, level=0):
  if linprog is None: return None
  n=a.size
  if n<=1 or n>350: return None
  M = float(np.max(fast_convolve(a)))
  if not finite or M<=0: return None
  rows=2*n-1
  A=np.zeros((rows,n), dtype=np.float64)
  for k in range(rows):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if lo<=hi:
         # row coefficients for g[lo:hi+1]
         A[k,lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
  c = -np.ones(n) - 1e-7*rng.random(n)  # maximize sum + random
  b = np.full(rows, float(M))
  try:
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 20.0), method='highs')
  except Exception:
    try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,20.0))
    except: return None
  if not res.success: return None
  g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
  g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
  g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
  G=float(g.sum())
  if G <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
      return None
  g/=G
  # ensure nonnegative
  return g
```
The LP may return a vector with sum much >1 due cap? Then rescale. If sum <=1 no gain.

Note: Objective minimize c = -sum - eps random. Since random nonneg, maximizing sum + eps random. Good. However constraints b=M exact. The feasible set includes a (sum1, equality constraints), so optimum G≥1 if a in feasible? Yes f*a coefficient =p≤M. So G≥1. If numerical no.

Low-dim search:
Top-level global `_LOW`.
```
def lowdim_objective(theta):
    t=_LOW['t']; n=int(_LOW['n']); mode=_LOW['mode']; n_ctl=_LOW['n_ctl']
    if mode=='free':
       th=np.asarray(theta,float)
       vals=np.exp(np.clip(th, -8.0, 8.0))
    else:
       th0=float(theta[0])
       delta=np.clip(theta[1:], 0.0, 6.0)
       th=np.empty(n_ctl)
       th[0]=th0
       acc=0.0
       for i in range(1,n_ctl):
          acc += float(delta[i-1])
          th[i]=th0-acc
       th=np.clip(th,-20,10)
       vals=np.exp(th)
    a=np.interp(t, np.linspace(0,1,n_ctl,endpoint=True), vals)
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    if a is None: return 1e100
    return score_fast_array(a)
```
For mono, if th very negative, underflow to zero; okay.

`run_de` top-level:
```
def run_de(bounds, seed, maxiter, popsize):
    if differential_evolution is None: return None
    try:
       return differential_evolution(lowdim_objective, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, tol=1e-5, disjoint=False, disp=False, polish=False, workers=1, initial='sobol')
    except Exception:
       try:

`lowdim_theta_to_a`: For mono, if theta[1:] length n_ctl-1. Use `when mode == 'free'`. Need access `_LOW['n_ctl']`. Code:
```
def lowdim_theta_to_a(theta):
    theta=np.asarray(theta, dtype=float).ravel()
    nctl=int(_LOW['n_ctl']); n=int(_LOW['n']); t_new=_LOW['t_new']; t_old=_LOW['t_old']
    if theta.size != nctl: return None
    if _LOW['mode'] == 'mono':
       th=np.empty(nctl)
       th[0]=float(theta[0])
       acc=0.0
       for i in range(1,nctl):
          d=float(theta[i])
          if d<0: d=0.0
          acc += d
          th[i]=th[0]-acc
       th=np.clip(th, -25.0, 15.0)
       vals=np.exp(th)
    else:
       th=np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)
       vals=np.exp(th)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
       vals=np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=1.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=1.0)
    vals=np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    if float(vals.sum()) <= 1e-300:
       vals=np.ones(nctl)
    a=np.interp(t_new, t_old, vals)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
`np.nan_to_num` parameters `posinf` named availability? yes. Could avoid: `vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 1.0)`. Simpler.
```
vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 1.0)
```

   if a is None: return 1e100

Now, `optimize_vector`: Need top-level. Include prints? It may be called multiple, printing each scale can be in propose. Inside, maybe no print to reduce. But can print final best at end? We'll print in propose.

Detailed `optimize_vector`:
```
def optimize_vector(a0, time_limit, rng, use_lp=True):
    a0=normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0 is None: return None, inf
    current=a0.copy()
    cur_s=score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))
    best=current.copy(); best_s=cur_s
    n=a0.size
    stop=time.monotonic()+max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    it=0; noimp=0; lp_calls=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)) or float(current.sum()) <= 1e-300:
            current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s
        p=fast_convolve(current)
        pmax=float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 0.0:
            current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s; continue
        mode = it % 3
        w=make_weights(p, mode)
        g=active_gradient(current,w)
        gmax=float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
            g=np.zeros(n); gmax=1.0
        gn=g/gmax
        improved=False
        factors = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0)
        if noimp > 8: factors = (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0)
        for fac in factors:
           z = -fac * gn
           z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
           cand = current * np.exp(z)
           cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
           if cand is None: continue
           conv=fast_convolve(cand)
           sc=score_fast_array(cand, conv)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_s - 1e-12:
              current=cand; cur_s=sc; improved=True
              if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                  best=cand.copy(); best_s=sc; noimp=0
              break
        if not improved:
           # linear
           for lr in (0.05,0.2,0.5):
              v=current - lr*gn
              v=project_simplex(v)
              v=normalize_sequence(v)
              if v is None: continue
              conv=fast_convolve(v); sc=score_fast_array(v, conv)
              if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_s - 1e-12:
                 current=v; cur_s=sc; improved=True
                 if sc < best_s -1e-12:
                    best=v.copy(); best_s=sc; noimp=0
                 break
           if not improved:
              noimp +=1
              if noimp % 5 == 0:
                 # blend toward best
                 if noimp > 8:
                    mix=normalize_sequence(0.35*current + 0.65*best)
                    if mix is not None:
                       current=mix; cur_s=score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))
                 current=perturb_sequence(current, min(0.8, 0.05*noimp+0.05), rng)
                 cur_s=score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))
                 if np.isfinite(cur_s) and cur_s < best_s:
                    best=current.copy(); best_s=cur_s
                 # reverse check
                 rev=best[::-1]
                 rs=score_fast_array(rev, fast_convolve(rev))
                 if np.isfinite(rs) and rs < best_s -1e-12:
                    best=rev; best_s=rs; current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s
              if use_lp and n <= 300 and lp_calls < 4 and noimp > 10 and (it % 25 == 0):
                 lp_calls +=1
                 gd=lp_direction(current, rng)
                 if gd is not None:
                    for mix in (0.05,0.1,0.3,0.6):
                       cand=normalize_sequence((1.0-mix)*current + mix*gd)
                       if cand is None: continue
                       conv=fast_convolve(cand); sc=score_fast_array(cand, conv)
                       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_s -1e-12:
                          current=cand; cur_s=sc; improved=True
                          if sc < best_s -1e-12:
                             best=cand.copy(); best_s=sc; noimp=0
                          break
        it +=1
    # final normalize
    best=normalize_sequence(best)
    if best is None: return None, inf
    return best, score_fast_array(best, fast_convolve(best))
```

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step-function heights.

The score is homogeneous, so most of the search keeps sum(a)=1 and minimizes
    2*n*max(a*a) / (sum(a)^2).

The algorithm combines:
  1) low-dimensional spline searches (DE / Powell) to discover a good envelope,
  2) coarse-to-fine vector optimization at increasing lengths,
  3) mirror-descent / projected-gradient steps driven by active convolution
     coefficients,
  4) optional LP "response" moves at moderate sizes,
  5) final exact evaluation of several saved candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

# Global scratch state for the low-dimensional spline objective.  This avoids
# nested closures while still allowing differential_evolution / Powell to call
# a top-level function with a single argument.
_LOW = {
    "n": 256,
    "n_ctl": 12,
    "mode": "free",
    "t_new": np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 256),
    "t_old": np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 12),
}


def normalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a clipped, finite, normalized copy of a if possible."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def parent_sequence():
    """Retrieve the supplied height_sequence_1, normalized, if valid."""
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s


def resize_sequence(a, n):
    """Resize an existing sequence to length n by interpolation/block averaging."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if m == 0:
        return None
    if m == n:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    if m > n:
        usable = (m // n) * n
        if usable <= 0:
            b = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n),
                          np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m), a)
            return normalize_sequence(b)
        b = a[:usable].reshape(-1, n).mean(axis=0)
        tail = a[usable:]
        if tail.size > 0:
            b = b + float(tail.sum()) / float(n)
        return normalize_sequence(b)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def fast_convolve(a):
    """Full self-convolution.  Uses direct convolution for moderate sizes."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    # Direct numpy convolution is exact and fast enough up to this size.
    if n <= 800:
        return np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")

    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    c = c[:need]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_fast_array(a, conv=None):
    """Fast approximate score (exact for n <= 800 with the direct convolution)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if conv is None:
        conv = fast_convolve(a)
    if conv.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(conv))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def exact_score(a):
    """Score using the authoritative evaluator."""
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in a]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def score_key(item):
    """Sort key for (score, sequence) pairs."""
    return float(item[0])


def project_simplex(v):
    """Project a vector onto the simplex sum(v)=1, v>=0, then normalize again."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssm = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * rho > (cssm - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssm[idx] - 1.0) / float(idx + 1)
    p = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    return p


def make_weights(p, mode):
    """
    Weights on convolution coefficients near the maximum.

    mode 0: broad (B=1)
    mode 1: medium (B=5)
    mode 2: sharp (B=25)
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(p))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w

    B = (1.0, 5.0, 25.0)[int(mode) % 3]
    e = -B * (m - p) / m
    e = np.clip(e, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(e)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(p))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def active_gradient(a, w):
    """
    Gradient vector g_i = sum_k w_k * a_{k-i}, where valid indices are used.

    This is the derivative of the weighted convolution sum with respect to a_i.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        if n == 1:
            return np.array([float(np.sum(w[:1]) * float(a[0])), ], dtype=np.float64)
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if n <= 200:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            aj = float(a[j])
            if aj != 0.0:
                g += aj * w[j:j + n]
        g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
        return g

    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, L)
    g = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
    return g


def perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng):
    """Random log-multiplicative block/trend perturbation, normalized."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    b = a.copy()
    max_width = max(2, min(n, n // 5))
    width = int(rng.integers(1, max_width + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
    b[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width))

    if rng.random() < 0.4:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        b *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6 * sigma) * t)

    if rng.random() < 0.18:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        b *= 1.0 + 0.25 * sigma * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * rng.uniform(1.0, 5.0) * t + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        )

    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def lp_direction(a, rng):
    """
    Optional LP Frank-Wolfe-like direction.

    Given current f, solve max sum(g) s.t. g>=0, f*g <= max(f*f).
    If a larger-sum feasible g exists, blending with it can help.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1 or n > 350:
        return None

    M = float(np.max(fast_convolve(a)))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    rows = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((rows, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(rows):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo <= hi:
            A[k, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

    # Maximize sum(g) with a tiny random tie-break to avoid staying exactly at f.
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - 1e-7 * rng.random(n)
    b = np.full(rows, M, dtype=np.float64)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 20.0), method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 20.0))
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    G = float(g.sum())
    if G <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
        return None
    g /= G
    return g


def optimize_vector(a0, time_limit, rng, use_lp=True):
    """
    Local search at fixed length.

    Uses mirror-descent steps derived from active-set gradients, projected
    linear steps, random restarts, and optional LP directions.
    """
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0 is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    current = a0.copy()
    current_score = score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))
    best = current.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    n = int(current.size)
    stop = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    it = 0
    noimp = 0
    lp_calls = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)) or float(current.sum()) <= 1e-300:
            current = best.copy()
            current_score = best_score

        p = fast_convolve(current)
        pmax = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 0.0:
            current = best.copy()
            current_score = best_score
            continue

        w = make_weights(p, it % 3)
        g = active_gradient(current, w)
        gmax = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            gmax = 1.0

        gn = g / gmax
        improved = False

        if noimp <= 8:
            factors = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0)
        else:
            factors = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)

        for fac in factors:
            z = np.clip(-fac * gn, -25.0, 25.0)
            cand = current * np.exp(z)
            cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            conv = fast_convolve(cand)
            sc = score_fast_array(cand, conv)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                current = cand
                current_score = sc
                improved = True
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    noimp = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            for lr in (0.05, 0.2, 0.5):
                v = current - lr * gn
                v = project_simplex(v)
                v = normalize_sequence(v)
                if v is None:
                    continue
                conv = fast_convolve(v)
                sc = score_fast_array(v, conv)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                    current = v
                    current_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best = v.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        noimp = 0
                    break

        if not improved:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp % 5 == 0:
                if noimp > 8:
                    mix = normalize_sequence(0.35 * current + 0.65 * best)
                    if mix is not None:
                        current = mix
                        current_score = score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))

                current = perturb_sequence(current, min(0.8, 0.05 * noimp + 0.05), rng)
                current_score = score_fast_array(current, fast_convolve(current))
                if np.isfinite(current_score) and current_score < best_score:
                    best = current.copy()
                    best_score = current_score

                rev = best[::-1]
                rs = score_fast_array(rev, fast_convolve(rev))
                if np.isfinite(rs) and rs < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = rev
                    best_score = rs
                    current = best.copy()
                    current_score = best_score

            if use_lp and n <= 300 and lp_calls < 4 and noimp > 10 and (it % 25 == 0):
                lp_calls += 1
                gd = lp_direction(current, rng)
                if gd is not None:
                    for mix in (0.05, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6):
                        cand = normalize_sequence((1.0 - mix) * current + mix * gd)
                        if cand is None:
                            continue
                        conv = fast_convolve(cand)
                        sc = score_fast_array(cand, conv)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                            current = cand
                            current_score = sc
                            improved = True
                            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best = cand.copy()
                                best_score = sc
                                noimp = 0
                            break

        it += 1

    best = normalize_sequence(best)
    if best is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    return best, score_fast_array(best, fast_convolve(best))


def lowdim_theta_to_a(theta):
    """Convert low-dimensional parameters into a normalized sequence."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    nctl = int(_LOW["n_ctl"])
    n = int(_LOW["n"])
    t_new = _LOW["t_new"]
    t_old = _LOW["t_old"]

    if theta.size != nctl:
        return None

    if _LOW["mode"] == "mono":
        th = np.empty(nctl, dtype=np.float64)
        th[0] = float(theta[0])
        acc = 0.0
        for i in range(1, nctl):
            d = float(theta[i])
            if d < 0.0:
                d = 0.0
            acc += d
            th[i] = th[0] - acc
        th = np.clip(th, -25.0, 12.0)
        vals = np.exp(th)
    else:
        th = np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)
        vals = np.exp(th)

    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 1.0)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    if float(vals.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        vals = np.ones(nctl, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.interp(t_new, t_old, vals)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def lowdim_objective(theta):
    """Scalar objective for low-dimensional spline search."""
    a = lowdim_theta_to_a(theta)
    if a is None:
        return 1e100
    sc = score_fast_array(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100
    return float(sc)


def run_de(bounds, seed, maxiter, popsize):
    """Run differential_evolution if available, with a fallback signature."""
    if differential_evolution is None:
        return None
    try:
        return differential_evolution(
            lowdim_objective,
            bounds,
            seed=seed,
            maxiter=maxiter,
            popsize=popsize,
            tol=1e-5,
            disp=False,
            polish=False,
            workers=1,
            initial="sobol",
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            return differential_evolution(
                lowdim_objective,
                bounds,
                seed=seed,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                popsize=popsize,
                tol=1e-5,
                disp=False,
                polish=False,
            )
        except Exception:
            return None


def polish_low_dim(x0, maxiter):
    """Polish a low-dimensional parameter vector with Powell if possible."""
    if minimize is None or x0 is None:
        return None
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lowdim_objective,
            x0,
            method="Powell",
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def low_dim_search(max_seconds, rng):
    """
    Search over smooth 12-control-point envelopes.

    Returns a few promising normalized sequences of length _LOW['n'].
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    out = []

    if max_seconds < 8.0:
        return out

    n = 256
    n_ctl = 12
    _LOW["n"] = n
    _LOW["n_ctl"] = n_ctl
    _LOW["t_new"] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    _LOW["t_old"] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_ctl)

    if max_seconds < 250.0:
        maxiter = 14
        popsize = 10
    elif max_seconds < 450.0:
        maxiter = 22
        popsize = 12
    else:
        maxiter = 32
        popsize = 15

    starts = []

    if differential_evolution is not None:
        _LOW["mode"] = "free"
        bounds_free = [(-5.0, 5.0)] * n_ctl
        seed = int(rng.integers(1, 1_000_000_000))
        res = run_de(bounds_free, seed, maxiter, popsize)
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            starts.append(("free", res.x))

        if time.monotonic() > start + max_seconds * 0.55:
            starts.append(("free", rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n_ctl)))
        else:
            _LOW["mode"] = "mono"
            # mono parameters: theta0 and nonnegative downward jumps
            bounds_mono = [(-2.0, 5.0)] + [(0.0, 3.0)] * (n_ctl - 1)
            seed2 = int(rng.integers(1, 1_000_000_000))
            res2 = run_de(bounds_mono, seed2, maxiter, popsize)
            if res2 is not None and hasattr(res2, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res2.x)):
                starts.append(("mono", res2.x))

    if not starts:
        for _ in range(3):
            starts.append(("free", rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n_ctl)))
        for _ in range(2):
            x = np.empty(n_ctl, dtype=np.float64)
            x[0] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
            x[1:] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0, n_ctl - 1)
            starts.append(("mono", x))

    polish_iters = 70 if max_seconds < 180 else 120

    # Add a few random starts as additional diverse basins.
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(("free", rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n_ctl)))
    for _ in range(1):
        x = np.empty(n_ctl, dtype=np.float64)
        x[0] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        x[1:] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0, n_ctl - 1)
        starts.append(("mono", x))

    for mode, x in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > start + max_seconds - 5.0:
            break
        _LOW["mode"] = mode
        try:
            xp = polish_low_dim(x, polish_iters)
        except Exception:
            xp = None
        use_x = xp if xp is not None else x
        a = lowdim_theta_to_a(use_x)
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)
            out.append(a[::-1])

    scored = []
    for a in out:
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = score_fast_array(a, fast_convolve(a))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), a))

    scored.sort(key=score_key)
    return [a for _, a in scored[:5]]


def initial_candidates(n, rng):
    """Diverse deterministic/random starting sequences of length n."""
    out = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        out.append(np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64))
        return out

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    scale = max(1.0, float(n) - 1.0)

    # Uniform with noise.
    for sig in (0.05, 0.15, 0.35):
        b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + sig * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        b = normalize_sequence(b)
        if b is not None:
            out.append(b)

    # Exponential decays/increases.
    for lam in (0.1, 0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0):
        b = np.exp(-lam * t)
        c = normalize_sequence(b)
        if c is not None:
            out.append(c)
            out.append(c[::-1])

    # Inverted powers.
    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        b = 1.0 / np.power(1.0 + 10.0 * (idx / scale), p)
        c = normalize_sequence(b)
        if c is not None:
            out.append(c)
            out.append(c[::-1])

    # Quadratic profiles.
    for c1 in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.2, 0.2, 0.6, 1.0):
        for c2 in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
            b = 1.0 + c1 * t + c2 * t * t
            if b.size and float(np.min(b)) > 1e-6:
                c = normalize_sequence(b)
                if c is not None:
                    out.append(c)
                    out.append(c[::-1])

    # Random piecewise-linear control points, free and monotone.
    m = 8
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)

    for _ in range(8):
        vals = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
        b = np.interp(t, xq, vals)
        c = normalize_sequence(b)
        if c is not None:
            out.append(c)
            out.append(c[::-1])

    for _ in range(8):
        th0 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        deltas = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m - 1)) * 0.8
        th = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
        th[0] = th0
        acc = 0.0
        for i in range(1, m):
            acc += float(deltas[i - 1])
            th[i] = th0 - acc
        vals = np.exp(np.clip(th, -6.0, 6.0))
        b = np.interp(t, xq, vals)
        c = normalize_sequence(b)
        if c is not None:
            out.append(c)
            out.append(c[::-1])

    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    print("propose_candidate: budget_s =", budget_s)

    parent = parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None:
        print("parent length:", parent.size,
              "parent fast score:", score_fast_array(parent, fast_convolve(parent)))
    else:
        print("no usable parent sequence")

    # 1. Low-dimensional shape discovery.
    low_budget = min(350.0, max(20.0, (deadline - start) * 0.15))
    low_shapes = low_dim_search(low_budget, rng)
    print("low-dim shapes:", len(low_shapes))

    # 2. Build a pool at the first vector scale.
    n0 = 256
    pool = []

    if parent is not None:
        pa = resize_sequence(parent, n0)
        if pa is not None:
            pool.append(pa)

    pool.extend(low_shapes)
    pool.extend(initial_candidates(n0, rng))

    scored = []
    for a in pool:
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = score_fast_array(a, fast_convolve(a))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), a))

    scored.sort(key=score_key)
    if scored:
        print("n0 best fast initial score:", scored[0][0])

    final_candidates = []

    # Use top-three starting shapes and polish at n0.
    results = []
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now)
    n0_budget = remaining * 0.20
    topk0 = min(3, len(scored)) if scored else 1
    for i in range(topk0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = n0_budget / float(max(1, topk0))
        limit = max(5.0, min(limit, deadline - now - 5.0))
        if limit <= 2.0:
            break
        a = scored[i][1] if i < len(scored) else normalize_sequence(np.ones(n0))
        b, s = optimize_vector(a, limit, rng, use_lp=True)
        if b is not None:
            b = normalize_sequence(b)
            s = score_fast_array(b, fast_convolve(b))
            if np.isfinite(s):
                results.append((float(s), b))
                final_candidates.append(b)

    if results:
        results.sort(key=score_key)
        print("n0 optimized best fast score:", results[0][0])

    # 3. Coarse-to-fine vector optimization.
    scales = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096]
    frac_alloc = [0.12, 0.20, 0.28, 0.40]

    for i, n in enumerate(scales):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        if remaining < 25.0 or not results:
            break

        sec = remaining * frac_alloc[i]
        if sec < 15.0:
            sec = remaining * 0.5

        topk = 2 if n <= 1024 else 1
        new_results = []

        for j in range(min(topk, len(results))):
            now = time.monotonic()
            limit = sec / float(max(1, topk))
            limit = max(5.0, min(limit, deadline - now - 5.0))
            if limit <= 2.0:
                break

            parent_a = results[j][1]
            a = resize_sequence(parent_a, n)
            if a is None:
                a = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n))

            b, s = optimize_vector(a, limit, rng, use_lp=(n <= 512))
            if b is None:
                b = a
            b = normalize_sequence(b)
            s = score_fast_array(b, fast_convolve(b))
            if np.isfinite(s):
                new_results.append((float(s), b))
                final_candidates.append(b)

        if not new_results:
            a = resize_sequence(results[0][1], n)
            if a is None:
                a = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n))
            s = score_fast_array(a, fast_convolve(a))
            if np.isfinite(s):
                new_results.append((float(s), a))
                final_candidates.append(a)

        new_results.sort(key=score_key)
        if new_results:
            results = new_results
            print("scale", n, "best fast score:", results[0][0])

    # 4. If useful time remains, refine the current best once more.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now)
    if remaining > 250.0 and results:
        limit = min(remaining * 0.6, 900.0)
        b, s = optimize_vector(results[0][1], limit, rng, use_lp=False)
        if b is not None:
            b = normalize_sequence(b)
            s = score_fast_array(b, fast_convolve(b))
            if np.isfinite(s):
                results.append((float(s), b))
                final_candidates.append(b)
                results.sort(key=score_key)
                print("extra refinement best fast score:", results[0][0])

    # 5. Exact evaluation of the shortlist.
    final_tuples = []
    for a in final_candidates:
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = score_fast_array(a, fast_convolve(a))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            final_tuples.append((float(sc), a))

    final_tuples.sort(key=score_key)

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_seq = None
    checked = 0
    max_check = max(8, min(18, len(final_tuples)))
    for sc, a in final_tuples[:max_check]:
        val = exact_score(a)
        checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_seq = [float(x) for x in a]

    if best_seq is not None:
        print("best exact score after checking", checked, "candidates:", best_exact)
        return best_seq

    # Fallback: return the best fast-scored candidate if evaluator was unusable.
    if final_tuples:
        a = normalize_sequence(final_tuples[0][1])
        if a is not None:
            return [float(x) for x in a]

    # Absolute fallback.
    return [1.0] * 256
```
